<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Interesting — the model files are actually present! 4/4 safetensors shards, all with reasonable sizes (3.4-3.7GB each, totaling ~14.3GB). The download timestamps show they completed between 02:37 and 02:55 on May 14.

So the model IS actually downloaded successfully to `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen2___5-7B/`. The worker reported `status=failed`, but the model is on disk.

The issue is likely that:
1. The code references `Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B` (HuggingFace format), but the model was downloaded via ModelScope to `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen2___5-7B/` (ModelScope format with `___` instead of `.`)


2. The worker probably didn't set `HF_HOME` correctly to point to this cache directory, or there's a path mismatch between what the code expects and where the model actually landed
3. The worker failure might stem from a failed HuggingFace download attempt (network or SSL issues) even though ModelScope succeeded, or possibly a validation error

The real question is whether `AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained("Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B")` can actually locate the model at that ModelScope cache path — HuggingFace's standard cache structure uses different naming conventions, so there's likely a path resolution problem here.

The fix would be to either load directly from the local path, configure ModelScope's cache environment variable, or set up a symlink to align with HF's expected layout. The model itself is there on disk, it just needs the right loading mechanism. This explains why the worker reported failure — it probably attempted a HuggingFace download that failed due to network issues.
```

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01YBiWSwgqNSm5gocG1Qp32W`</summary>

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/ratekv/ROADMAP.md\", \"content\": \"# ratekv — Research Dashboard\\n\\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-13 19:47 UTC\\n\\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-13 19:10 UTC | 更新原因: 同步 pass/fail 标准与代码实际逻辑；模型切换 Qwen2.5-7B；mvp_001 已启动\\n\\n## 核心方向\\n联合优化 KV cache eviction 和 quantization 的 per-KV-head 分配：通过 rate-distortion 理论将两者统一为 MCKP (Multiple-Choice Knapsack Problem)，在 matched memory budget 下为每个 KV head 选择最优压缩策略。Training-free, FA-compatible, input-adaptive。\\n\\n## 当前阶段: MVP 验证\\n\\n**核心假设**: 在 per-KV-head 粒度上，eviction 和 quantization 存在 crossover——部分 head（sparse attention）在 matched memory budget 下 eviction distortion < quantization distortion，**且 joint eviction+quantization allocation 优于 quantization-only optimal allocation**。\\n\\n**MVP 实验设计**:\\n- 模型: Qwen2.5-7B (4 KV heads/layer × 28 layers = 112 MCKP decisions)（原计划 Llama 3.1-8B，因 HF gated 切换，见 D003）\\n- Calibration: C4 subset (16 seq × 512 tokens)\\n- Eval: WikiText-2 perplexity (non-overlapping stride=512)\\n- Distortion metric: per-KV-head attention output MSE\\n- 步骤:\\n  1. Per-KV-head distortion profiling (eviction: attention-score-based @ retention {1/16, 1/8, 1/4, 1/2}; quantization: per-channel key/per-token value @ {2, 4, 8} bit)\\n  2. Crossover 可视化 (eviction vs quantization distortion at memory-equivalent points)\\n  3. MCKP solver (LP-relaxation-guided greedy)\\n  4. 三路对比: full MCKP (eviction+quantization) vs quant-only MCKP vs uniform 4-bit quantization\\n\\n**Pass/Fail 标准** (v2, 与代码一致):\\n- PASS: ≥10% KV heads 在至少一个 compression ratio 下 prefer eviction，且 full MCKP PPL 优于 quant-only MCKP ≥0.5 PPL\\n- FAIL: <10% heads prefer eviction，或 full MCKP 未能优于 quant-only MCKP ≥0.5 PPL\\n\\n## 当前进展\\n- mvp_001 已在 cuda:0 运行中 (Qwen2.5-7B, PID 128388)，预计 1-2h 完成\\n- cuda:1 sanity check (Qwen2.5-1.5B) 已派发，验证 pipeline 正确性\\n- 环境搭建完成，代码 6 文件已部署 (profiler/solver/eval_ppl/visualize/run_mvp/utils)\\n- GPU zombie 进程已清理，2× RTX PRO 6000 96GB 全部可用\\n\\n## 近期任务\\n- [ ] 等 mvp_001 完成，分析 crossover + PPL 结果\\n- [ ] cuda:1 sanity check (Qwen2.5-1.5B) 验证 pipeline\\n- [ ] MVP 结果出来后判定 pass/fail，规划后续\\n\\n## GPU 预算\\nwestd-37397: 2× RTX PRO 6000 96GB (cuda:0 跑 mvp_001, cuda:1 跑 sanity check)\\nMVP 预计总计 <2 GPU-hours\\n\\n## 投稿目标\\nARR May 25 (EMNLP 2026) 极度紧张（12 天）。备选: AAAI 2027 (Aug) / ICLR 2027 (Sep)。MVP 结果出来后再定。\\n\\n## 关键风险\\n1. 与 RateQuant 的 incrementality——需通过 Proposition (waterfilling 在异构 action space 下失效) + crossover empirical analysis 差异化\\n2. Joint allocation 实际增益幅度不确定——gain decomposition 作为叙事后路\\n3. Qwen2.5-7B 的 GQA ratio (7:1) 较高，attention pattern 可能更平均化，crossover 比例可能低于预期\\n\\n## MVP 后续路径规划\\n**PASS 路径**: 进入 research 阶段 → 写 paper_framing.md + experiment_plan.yaml → dream_simulate → 扩模型 (加 Llama/Mistral) + 扩 benchmark + ablation\\n**FAIL 路径**: 分析失败原因 → (a) crossover 不存在: 假设被否定，pivot 或归档; (b) crossover 存在但 MCKP 增益 <0.5 PPL: 检查 solver/distortion metric 是否有改进空间，考虑换 distortion metric 或调 action set 粒度\\n\\n## 关键决策日志\\n\\n| ID | 日期 | 摘要 | 关联实验 |\\n|----|------|------|----------|\\n| D003 | 2026-05-13 | MVP 模型从 Llama 3.1-8B 切换到 Qwen2.5-7B，DuoAttention v | — |\\n| D002 | 2026-05-13 | MVP gate v2: 加 quant-only MCKP baseline + 消除死区 + 最 | — |\\n| D001 | 2026-05-13 | MVP 验证方案: Llama 3.1-8B 上验证 eviction-quantization c | — |\\n\\n\\n\", \"lines\": 65}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
```

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/ratekv/decisions.yaml\", \"content\": \"_next_id: 4\\ndecisions:\\n- id: D001\\n  date: 2026-05-13 17:13 UTC\\n  summary: 'MVP 验证方案: Llama 3.1-8B 上验证 eviction-quantization crossover 存在性 + MCKP\\n    分配有效性'\\n  rationale: '核心假设是 per-KV-head 异质性使得 joint eviction+quantization allocation 优于 uniform\\n    quantization。最小验证: 单模型 distortion profiling + crossover 可视化 + MCKP vs uniform\\n    4-bit 对比。Pass 标准: ≥15% heads prefer eviction + perplexity improvement。选 Llama\\n    3.1-8B 因为 DuoAttention 已有该模型的 streaming/retrieval head 分类数据可做 post-hoc validation。'\\n  related_experiments: []\\n  depends_on: []\\n- id: D002\\n  date: 2026-05-13 17:16 UTC\\n  summary: 'MVP gate v2: 加 quant-only MCKP baseline + 消除死区 + 最小效应量 ≥0.5 PPL'\\n  rationale: 'Reviewer 初审指出三个漏洞: (1) uniform 4-bit 太弱，任何 per-head 方法都能赢，核心 claim 是\\n    joint > quant-only，必须加 quant-only MCKP baseline; (2) 5%-15% crossover 比例死区导致判定模糊，合并为单一\\n    ≥10% 阈值; (3) PPL 改善无最小效应量可能产生假阳性，设 ≥0.5 PPL 门槛。quant-only baseline 零额外工程量（同一 solver\\n    限制 action set）。'\\n  related_experiments: []\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D001\\n- id: D003\\n  date: 2026-05-13 18:12 UTC\\n  summary: MVP 模型从 Llama 3.1-8B 切换到 Qwen2.5-7B，DuoAttention validation 延后\\n  rationale: Llama 3.1-8B 是 HF gated model，用户尚未提供 HF token，GPU 空闲不能等。Qwen2.5-7B 非\\n    gated、GQA 架构、7B 参数量相近，满足 MVP 验证 per-KV-head crossover 假设的需求。代价：DuoAttention post-hoc\\n    validation（D001 rationale 中的 convergent evidence story）暂时无法做——DuoAttention 论文只分析了\\n    Llama 系列。替代方案：(1) 用户后续提供 HF token 后加 Llama 作为第二模型；(2) 在 Qwen 上自行运行 DuoAttention\\n    分类作为替代验证。MVP 核心假设（crossover 存在性 + MCKP 优于 uniform）不依赖 DuoAttention 叙事。注意：Qwen2.5-7B\\n    的 KV head 数（4×28=112）少于 Llama（8×32=256），GQA ratio 更高（7:1 vs 4:1），crossover 比例可能偏低。\\n  related_experiments: []\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D001\\n\", \"total_chars\": 1667, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
```

### Assistant

_source line 378 · 2026-05-13T19:48:01.200Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Clear. The experiment crashed because:

1. Model loaded successfully from local path
2. But `load_dataset("allenai/c4", "en", split="train", streaming=True)` failed because:
   - HuggingFace Hub is unreachable (`[Errno 101] Network is unreachable`)
   - This is an AutoDL instance — the `source /etc/network_turbo` proxy is set in `.bashrc`, but tmux sessions launched by the agent might not source `.bashrc` (non-interactive shell)
   - Even with proxy, the code tries to reach `huggingface.co` directly instead of through `hf-mirror.com`
   - The C4 dataset isn't cached locally either, so there's no fallback

The fix requires either pre-caching the C4 dataset, setting the `HF_ENDPOINT` environment variable in the tmux session itself, or switching to a locally available dataset. The misleading `EXIT_CODE=0` and the registry still showing `RUNNING` despite the process dying suggests the wrapper is masking the actual failure.

I need to update the registry to mark this as failed and post the findings.
```

</details>

### Assistant

_source line 407 · 2026-05-13T19:59:04.546Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
- WikiText: 307MB — looks complete (WikiText-2-raw-v1 test set is small, the full dataset including train/val/test is ~307MB, reasonable).
- C4: Only 48KB — this is just metadata, not the actual data! C4 is a streaming dataset (the code uses `streaming=True`), so only the metadata/README needs to be cached for the HF hub resolver to find it. But the actual data is still streamed from the internet.

Wait, let me reconsider. The code in profiler.py uses `load_dataset("allenai/c4", "en", split="train", streaming=True)`. 

With streaming enabled, the metadata gets cached locally, so the hub resolver should find the dataset without network access. But then the streaming iterator still needs to fetch actual data chunks from the internet, which will fail if there's no network connectivity.

The real issue is that C4's data URLs are embedded in the dataset metadata and point to HuggingFace's CDN. Even if the `HF_ENDPOINT` environment variable is set to use a mirror, tmux sessions won't have that variable configured, so the data streaming will still try to reach the original servers and fail.

The proper solution would be to ensure environment variables are set when launching experiments in tmux, since pre-caching metadata alone won't solve the network access problem for the actual data chunks. I should check whether the worker already modified the tmux setup or the profiler code to handle this.
```

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Per-KV-head distortion profiling for eviction and quantization.\\nInput: Qwen2.5-7B model + C4 calibration data (16 seq x 512 tokens)\\nOutput: JSON file with per-head distortion profiles\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\nfrom transformers.models.qwen2.modeling_qwen2 import apply_rotary_pos_emb\\nfrom utils import quantize_per_channel, quantize_per_token\\n\\n\\nclass DistortionProfiler:\\n    def __init__(self, model, tokenizer, device=\\\"cuda:0\\\", n_calibration_seqs=16, seq_len=512, calibration_data=None):\\n        self.model = model\\n        self.tokenizer = tokenizer\\n        self.device = device\\n        self.n_calibration_seqs = n_calibration_seqs\\n        self.seq_len = seq_len\\n        self.calibration_data = calibration_data\\n\\n    def _load_calibration_data(self):\\n        import os\\n        if self.calibration_data and os.path.exists(self.calibration_data):\\n            with open(self.calibration_data) as f:\\n                samples = json.load(f)\\n            all_ids = []\\n            for s in samples:\\n                tokens = self.tokenizer.encode(s[\\\"text\\\"], add_special_tokens=False)\\n                if len(tokens) >= self.seq_len:\\n                    all_ids.append(tokens[:self.seq_len])\\n                if len(all_ids) >= self.n_calibration_seqs:\\n                    break\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.43}}"}

The player progresses through a series of linear missions , gradually unlocked as maps that can be freely scanned through and replayed as they are unlocked . The route to each story location on the map varies depending on an individual player 's approach : when one option is selected , the other is sealed off to the player . Outside missions , the player characters rest in a camp , where units can be customized and character growth occurs . Alongside the main story missions are character @-@ specific sub missions relating to different squad members . After the game 's completion , additional episodes are unlocked , some of them having a higher difficulty than those found in the rest of the game . There are also love simulation elements related to the game 's two main heroines , although they take a very minor role . \\\\n\\\"}, {\\\"text\\\": \\\" The game 's battle system , the BliTZ system , is carried over directly from Valkyira Chronicles . A character can only act once per @-@ turn , but characters can be granted multiple turns at the expense of other characters ' turns . Each character has a field and distance of movement limited by their Action Gauge . Up to nine characters can be assigned to a single mission . During gameplay , characters will call out if something happens to them , such as their health points ( HP ) getting low or being knocked out by enemy attacks . Stories are told through comic book @-@ like panels with animated character portraits , with characters speaking partially through voiced speech bubbles and partially through unvoiced text . The player progresses through a series of linear missions , gradually unlocked as maps that can be freely scanned through and replayed as they are unlocked . The route to each story location on the map varies depending on an individual player 's approach : when one option is selected , the other is sealed off to the player . Outside missions , the player characters rest in a camp , where units can be customized and character growth occurs . Alongside the main story missions are character @-@ specific sub missions relating to different squad members . After the game 's completion , additional episodes are unlocked , some of them having a higher difficulty than those found in the rest of the game . There are also love simulation elements related to the game 's two main heroines , although they take a very minor role . \\\\n\\\"}, {\\\"text\\\": \\\" The game 's battle system , the BliTZ system , is carried over directly from Valkyira Chronicles . During missions , players select each unit using a top @-@ down perspective of the battlefield map : once a character is selected , the player moves the character around the battlefield in third @-@ person . A character can only act once per @-@ turn , but characters can be granted multiple turns at the expense of other characters ' turns . Each character has a field and distance of movement limited by their Action Gauge . Up to nine characters can be assigned to a single mission . During gameplay , characters will call out if something happens to them , such as their health points ( HP ) getting low or being knocked out by enemy attacks . Each character has specific \\\\\\\" Potentials \\\\\\\" , skills unique to each character . They are divided\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 59270 字符] ...\\n\\ne Timor and Arafura Seas . There are unsubstantiated reports that its range extends to southern Papua New Guinea . It is the least common of the several maskray species native to the region . This species is a bottom @-@ dweller that prefers habitats with fine sediment . It has been recorded from between 12 and 62 m ( 39 and 203 ft ) deep , and tends to be found farther away from shore than other maskrays in its range . \\\\n\\\"}, {\\\"text\\\": \\\" The plain maskray generally hunts at the surface of the bottom substrate , rather than digging for prey . Its diet consists predominantly of caridean shrimp and polychaete worms . Small bony fishes are also eaten , along with the occasional penaeid prawn or amphipod . Larger rays consume a greater variety of prey and relatively more polychaete worms when compared to smaller rays . This species is parasitized by the tapeworm Acanthobothrium jonesi . \\\\n\\\"}, {\\\"text\\\": \\\" Like other stingrays , the plain maskray is viviparous with the developing embryos sustained to term by histotroph ( \\\\\\\" uterine milk \\\\\\\" ) produced by the mother . Mature females have a single functional ovary and uterus , on the left . Litter size is one or two ; the newborns measure 12 \\\\u2013 14 cm ( 4 @.@ 7 \\\\u2013 5 @.@ 5 in ) across . Males and females reach sexual maturity at disc widths of 20 \\\\u2013 21 cm ( 7 @.@ 9 \\\\u2013 8 @.@ 3 in ) and 18 \\\\u2013 19 cm ( 7 @.@ 1 \\\\u2013 7 @.@ 5 in ) respectively . The maximum lifespan is estimated to be 9 years for males and 13 years for females . \\\\n\\\"}, {\\\"text\\\": \\\" The main conservation threat to the plain maskray is incidental capture by commercial bottom trawl fisheries . In the present day , this is mostly caused by Australia 's Northern Prawn Fishery , which operates throughout its range . Although this species is discarded when caught , it is more delicate @-@ bodied than other maskrays and is thus unlikely to survive encounters with trawling gear . Historically , this species may also have been negatively affected by Japanese , Chinese , and Taiwanese trawlers that fished intensively off northern Australia from 1959 to 1990 . These factors , coupled with the plain maskray 's limited distribution and low reproductive rate , have resulted in its being assessed as Near Threatened by the International Union for Conservation of Nature ( IUCN ) . \\\\n\\\"}, {\\\"text\\\": \\\" The 2011 \\\\u2013 12 Columbus Blue Jackets season was the team 's 12th season in the National Hockey League ( NHL ) . The Blue Jackets ' record of 29 \\\\u2013 46 \\\\u2013 7 [ note 1 ] was the worst record in the NHL for 2011 \\\\u2013 12 and the first time in franchise history they finished in last place . It also marked the third straight year that they missed the playoffs . With the prospect of another rebuild looming the Blue Jackets ' captain and best player , Rick Nash , requested to be traded , though he would remain with the team for the entire season . \\\\n\\\"}, {\\\"text\\\": \\\" The team was involved in a controversial loss to the Los Angeles Kings , when the Staples Center clock appeared to freeze at 1 @.@ 8 seconds allowing the Kings time to score the tying goal , before winning in overtime . For a proper paper, they'd want to redo this with C4 calibration to show true cross-domain validation. The fact that the calibration data starts with "Senjō no Valkyria 3," a well-known WikiText-2 document, confirms they're working within the same distribution.

Looking at the code logic, the profiler checks if a local calibration file exists and loads it, otherwise it falls back to streaming C4 — so the Agent would need to explicitly pass the calibration file path when running, otherwise it'll still attempt the C4 download.

_source line 453 · 2026-05-13T20:33:10.515Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Two different failures:

**sanity_002**: Trivial bug — `--output sanity_results.json` is not a valid argument for `run_mvp.py`. The script doesn't have an `--output` parameter. Easy fix.

**mvp_002**: More serious — `IndexError: shape mismatch: indexing tensors could not be broadcast together with shapes [0], [3584]`. This is a tensor indexing error deep in PyTorch's vmap/autograd machinery. The `[0]` suggests an empty tensor (0 elements) being used as an index alongside a tensor of shape [3584] (which is Qwen2.5-7B's hidden_size).


I need to dig into the mvp_002 logs to trace where this indexing error is actually happening in the code.
```

</details>

_source line 459 · 2026-05-13T20:33:27.907Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

````text
The mvp_002 error is in the causal mask creation within the Transformers library. The traceback shows:

```
File ".../transformers/models/qwen2/modeling_qwen2.py", line 372, in forward
    "full_attention": create_causal_mask(**mask_kwargs),
File ".../transformers/masking_utils.py", line 825, in create_causal_mask
    causal_mask = mask_interface(...)
File ".../transformers/masking_utils.py", line 508, in eager_mask


```

The issue occurs when the mask creation function tries to broadcast tensors with incompatible shapes—specifically `[0]` and `[3584]` (Qwen2.5-7B's hidden size)—during the initial forward pass in profiler.py. This shape mismatch happens in the vmap operation that's supposed to handle batch, head, query, and key vector dimensions together.

The root cause is likely a compatibility issue between the installed transformers version (probably 4.50+) and how the model is being invoked. The newer masking system might not handle `use_cache=False` correctly, or `cache_position` isn't being passed properly. I could try explicitly providing `cache_position` when calling the model, downgrading transformers to before the masking refactor, or switching attention implementations—though that last option won't work since the code needs eager attention to access weight matrices.
````

</details>

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Per-KV-head distortion profiling for eviction and quantization.\\nInput: Qwen2.5-7B model + C4 calibration data (16 seq x 512 tokens)\\nOutput: JSON file with per-head distortion profiles\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\nfrom transformers.models.qwen2.modeling_qwen2 import apply_rotary_pos_emb\\nfrom utils import quantize_per_channel, quantize_per_token\\n\\n\\nclass DistortionProfiler:\\n    def __init__(self, model, tokenizer, device=\\\"cuda:0\\\", n_calibration_seqs=16, seq_len=512, calibration_data=None):\\n        self.model = model\\n        self.tokenizer = tokenizer\\n        self.device = device\\n        self.n_calibration_seqs = n_calibration_seqs\\n        self.seq_len = seq_len\\n        self.calibration_data = calibration_data\\n\\n    def _load_calibration_data(self):\\n        import os\\n        if self.calibration_data and os.path.exists(self.calibration_data):\\n            with open(self.calibration_data) as f:\\n                samples = json.load(f)\\n            all_ids = []\\n            for s in samples:\\n                tokens = self.tokenizer.encode(s[\\\"text\\\"], add_special_tokens=False)\\n                if len(tokens) >= self.seq_len:\\n                    all_ids.append(tokens[:self.seq_len])\\n                if len(all_ids) >= self.n_calibration_seqs:\\n                    break\\n            return torch.tensor(all_ids, dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\\n        from datasets import load_dataset\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"allenai/c4\\\", \\\"en\\\", split=\\\"train\\\", streaming=True)\\n        all_ids = []\\n        for example in ds:\\n            if len(all_ids) >= self.n_calibration_seqs:\\n                break\\n            tokens = self.tokenizer.encode(example[\\\"text\\\"], add_special_tokens=False)\\n            if len(tokens) >= self.seq_len:\\n                all_ids.append(tokens[:self.seq_len])\\n        return torch.tensor(all_ids, dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\\n\\n    def profile(self):\\n        model = self.model\\n        device = self.device\\n        seq_len = self.seq_len\\n\\n        config = model.config\\n        num_layers = config.num_hidden_layers\\n        num_kv_heads = config.num_key_value_heads\\n        num_q_heads = config.num_attention_heads\\n        head_dim = config.hidden_size // num_q_heads\\n        gqa_ratio = num_q_heads // num_kv_heads\\n\\n        input_ids = self._load_calibration_data()\\n        if input_ids.numel() == 0 or (input_ids.dim() >= 1 and input_ids.shape[0] == 0):\\n            raise RuntimeError(\\n                f\\\"No calibration sequences loaded. Provide --calibration_data pointing to a JSON file \\\"\\n                f\\\"with [{{'text': ...}}] entries, each having >= {self.seq_len} tokens.\\\"\\n            )\\n        batch_size = input_ids.shape[0]\\n        print(f\\\"Calibration data: {batch_size} sequences x {seq_len} tokens\\\")\\n\\n        # Capture hidden_states at each attention layer via pre-hooks\\n        captured = {}\\n        hooks = []\\n\\n        def make_hook(idx):\\n            def hook_fn(module, args, kwargs):\\n                hs = kwargs.get(\\\"hidden_states\\\")\\n                if hs is None and len(args) > 0:\\n                    hs = args[0]\\n                captured[idx] = hs.detach().clone()\\n            return hook_fn\\n\\n        for i in range(num_layers):\\n            h = model.model.layers[i].self_attn.register_forward_pre_hook(\\n                make_hook(i), with_kwargs=True\\n            )\\n            hooks.append(h)\\n\\n        print(\\\"Running forward pass to capture hidden states...\\\")\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            model(input_ids, use_cache=False)\\n\\n        for h in hooks:\\n            h.remove()\\n\\n        # Position embeddings (shared across all layers)\\n        position_ids = torch.arange(seq_len, device=device).unsqueeze(0)\\n        model_dtype = next(model.parameters()).dtype\\n        dummy = torch.empty(1, device=device, dtype=model_dtype)\\n        cos, sin = model.model.rotary_emb(dummy, position_ids)\\n\\n        # Causal mask: -inf above diagonal\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}

_source line 564 · 2026-05-13T21:29:16.946Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Fifth round of failures!

**mvp_003**: `RuntimeError: No calibration sequences loaded. Provide --calibration_data pointing to a JSON file with [{'text': ...}] entries, each having >= 512 tokens.`

This is the validation check that was added in the previous fix. The command uses `--calibration_data /root/ratekv/c4_calibration.json`, so the file should be found. The issue is that the calibration data (extracted from WikiText) has samples that are too short — none of them have >= 512 tokens when tokenized. Let me think about this...


Actually, I'm realizing the c4_calibration.json entries came from WikiText-2, which are individual paragraphs and articles. Most of them are probably shorter than 512 tokens. The profiler needs 16 sequences that each meet the 512-token threshold, and if none of the 100 samples qualify, the calibration data ends up empty. But wait — I saw earlier that some entries in the file were quite long (the output was truncated showing many characters). Those longer entries should definitely have enough tokens. Let me look at the tokenization logic more carefully...

Checking the code...

The tokenizer encodes each sample's text and only keeps sequences that are at least 512 tokens long, collecting them until reaching 16 sequences. The problem might be that the Qwen tokenizer is producing fewer tokens per text than expected, or the JSON entries are actually individual paragraphs that are too short. Looking back at the c4_calibration.json, the first entry about "Senjō no Valkyria 3" was quite long, but WikiText-2 splits articles into paragraphs separated by newlines. Each JSON entry might just be a single paragraph with only 100-200 tokens, even though the overall file is massive.

The real issue is likely that the calibration data comes from WikiText-2, where most entries are short paragraphs rather than full articles, so the tokenizer can't find enough sequences that meet the 512-token minimum threshold.

For the sanity_003 error, the path contains triple underscores from ModelScope's encoding of dots, which HuggingFace's validation rejects when treating it as a repo ID. Interestingly, the 7B model loaded fine from a similar path earlier, so there might be something specific about how the 1.5B model is being handled. the 1.5B model path might not exist at all since only the 7B was actually downloaded. Looking at the sanity_003 error more carefully, when `AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained()` can't find the directory, it falls back to treating it as a repo ID and fails validation. So I'm seeing two distinct issues: the calibration data in mvp_003 is too sparse for long sequences, and the 1.5B model simply wasn't downloaded to that cache location.

Now I need to systematically review all the code files to understand the full scope of what's happening.
```

### User

_source line 579 · 2026-05-13T21:31:47.915Z_

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   I am the independent scientific Reviewer for the `ratekv` project (RateKV: Input-Adaptive Rate-Distortion Allocation for Joint KV Cache Eviction and Quantization). My role is to independently verify experiment conclusions, review MVP design, audit code correctness, and post findings to Director via `reviewer_post_to_director`. The most recent request is a system-triggered comprehensive pipeline reliability review after 5 consecutive launch failures, asking me to evaluate: (1) remaining crash points after cache_position fix, (2) result credibility if experiments succeed, and (3) MVP gate caveats.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **RateKV**: Training-free framework jointly optimizing per-KV-head eviction and quantization via rate-distortion theory
   - **MCKP (Multiple-Choice Knapsack Problem)**: Formulation treating eviction and quantization as alternative compression strategies at memory-equivalent operating points
   - **Per-KV-head distortion profiling**: Measuring attention output MSE for eviction (retention fractions 1/16, 1/8, 1/4, 1/2) and quantization (2, 4, 8 bit) at each KV head
   - **GQA (Grouped Query Attention)**: Qwen2.5-7B has 4 KV heads/layer × 28 layers = 112 MCKP decisions, GQA ratio 7:1
   - **LP-relaxation-guided greedy**: MCKP solver builds Pareto front per head, then greedily downgrades from full precision
   - **Crossover analysis**: Comparing eviction vs quantization distortion at memory-equivalent points (e.g., evict(1/4)@16-bit vs quant(4-bit))
   - **Three-way comparison**: Full MCKP (eviction+quantization) vs quant-only MCKP vs uniform 4-bit
   - **AutoDL instance specifics**: Requires `source /etc/network_turbo` for proxy, HF mirror at `hf-mirror.com`, SSL certs, root partition only 30GB

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`/root/ratekv/profiler.py`**
     - Core distortion profiling logic. Captures hidden states via pre-hooks, computes Q/K/V projections per layer, measures MSE between baseline and compressed attention output for each KV head.
     - Modified: Added `calibration_data` parameter for local JSON loading, added empty data validation, changed import to `qwen2.modeling_qwen2`, added `cache_position` to model call.
     - Key line 89: `model(input_ids, use_cache=False, cache_position=torch.arange(input_ids.shape[1], device=input_ids.device))`
     - Current crash point: calibration data entries too short for 512 tokens when tokenized by Qwen tokenizer.

   - **`/root/ratekv/solver.py`**
     - MCKP solver implementation. `_build_pareto()` constructs Pareto-optimal action set per head. `solve_mckp()` does greedy downgrade. Supports `mode="full"` (eviction+quantization) and `mode="quant_only"`.
     - Memory equivalence: evict(f) at f=b/16 has identical memory cost to quant(b).
     - `uniform_quantization_baseline()` for naive comparison.
     - No modifications after initial deployment. Logic appears correct.

   - **`/root/ratekv/eval_ppl.py`**
     - `patch_attention()`: Monkey-patches model attention with per-head compression (quantization via utils functions, eviction via attention masking + re-softmax).
     - `evaluate_perplexity()`: Non-overlapping stride=512 on WikiText-2 test set.
     - Modified: Changed import to qwen2, added `cache_position` to model call.
     - Key line 134: `outputs = model(chunk, use_cache=False, cache_position=torch.arange(chunk.shape[1], device=chunk.device))`
     - Eviction in eval uses full softmax then masks + re-softmax (not FA-compatible, acceptable for MVP).

   - **`/root/ratekv/run_mvp.py`**
     - Orchestrator: profile → crossover analysis → MCKP solve (3 budgets: 0.5, 0.25, 0.125) → PPL eval → visualization.
     - Modified: Model default changed to `Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B`, added `--output` and `--calibration_data` args, heatmap dimensions now dynamic from model.config.
     - Pass criteria: `any_crossover` (≥10% heads prefer eviction) AND `pass_ppl` (quant_only_ppl - full_mckp_ppl ≥ 0.5).

   - **`/root/ratekv/utils.py`**
     - `quantize_per_channel()`: Symmetric uniform quantization per channel (dim=-2, across sequence). For keys.
     - `quantize_per_token()`: Symmetric uniform quantization per token (dim=-1, across head_dim). For values.
     - Matches KIVI scheme. No modifications after initial deployment.

   - **`/root/ratekv/visualize.py`**
     - `plot_crossover()`: Scatter plots of eviction vs quantization distortion at 3 memory-equivalent points, colored by layer.
     - `plot_ppl_comparison()`: Bar chart comparing PPL across methods.
     - `plot_allocation_heatmap()`: Layer × Head heatmap of allocation decisions.

        "content": "# ratekv — Research Dashboard\n\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-13 19:47 UTC\n\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-13 19:10 UTC | 更新原因: 同步 pass/fail 标准与代码实际逻辑；模型切换 Qwen2.5-7B；mvp_001 已启动\n\n## 核心方向\n联合优化 KV cache eviction 和 quantization 的 per-KV-head 分配：通过 rate-distortion 理论将两者统一为 MCKP (Multiple-Choice Knapsack Problem)，在 matched memory budget 下为每个 KV head 选择最优压缩策略。Training-free, FA-compatible, input-adaptive。\n\n## 当前阶段: MVP 验证\n\n**核心假设**: 在 per-KV-head 粒度上，eviction 和 quantization 存在 crossover——部分 head（sparse attention）在 matched memory budget 下 eviction distortion < quantization distortion，**且 joint eviction+quantization allocation 优于 quantization-only optimal allocation**。\n\n**MVP 实验设计**:\n- 模型: Qwen2.5-7B (4 KV heads/layer × 28 layers = 112 MCKP decisions)（原计划 Llama 3.1-8B，因 HF gated 切换，见 D003）\n- Calibration: C4 subset (16 seq × 512 tokens)\n- Eval: WikiText-2 perplexity (non-overlapping stride=512)\n- Distortion metric: per-KV-head attention output MSE\n- 步骤:\n  1. Per-KV-head distortion profiling (eviction: attention-score-based @ retention {1/16, 1/8, 1/4, 1/2}; quantization: per-channel key/per-token value @ {2, 4, 8} bit)\n  2. Crossover 可视化 (eviction vs quantization distortion at memory-equivalent points)\n  3. MCKP solver (LP-relaxation-guided greedy)\n  4. 三路对比: full MCKP (eviction+quantization) vs quant-only MCKP vs uniform 4-bit quantization\n\n**Pass/Fail 标准** (v2, 与代码一致):\n- PASS: ≥10% KV heads 在至少一个 compression ratio 下 prefer eviction，且 full MCKP PPL 优于 quant-only MCKP ≥0.5 PPL\n- FAIL: <10% heads prefer eviction，或 full MCKP 未能优于 quant-only MCKP ≥0.5 PPL\n\n## 当前进展\n- mvp_001 已在 cuda:0 运行中 (Qwen2.5-7B, PID 128388)，预计 1-2h 完成\n- cuda:1 sanity check (Qwen2.5-1.5B) 已派发，验证 pipeline 正确性\n- 环境搭建完成，代码 6 文件已部署 (profiler/solver/eval_ppl/visualize/run_mvp/utils)\n- GPU zombie 进程已清理，2× RTX PRO 6000 96GB 全部可用\n\n## 近期任务\n- [ ] 等 mvp_001 完成，分析 crossover + PPL 结果\n- [ ] cuda:1 sanity check (Qwen2.5-1.5B) 验证 pipeline\n- [ ] MVP 结果出来后判定 pass/fail，规划后续\n\n## GPU 预算\nwestd-37397: 2× RTX PRO 6000 96GB (cuda:0 跑 mvp_001, cuda:1 跑 sanity check)\nMVP 预计总计 <2 GPU-hours\n\n## 投稿目标\nARR May 25 (EMNLP 2026) 极度紧张（12 天）。备选: AAAI 2027 (Aug) / ICLR 2027 (Sep)。MVP 结果出来后再定。\n\n## 关键风险\n1. 与 RateQuant 的 incrementality——需通过 Proposition (waterfilling 在异构 action space 下失效) + crossover empirical analysis 差异化\n2. Joint allocation 实际增益幅度不确定——gain decomposition 作为叙事后路\n3. Qwen2.5-7B 的 GQA ratio (7:1) 较高，attention pattern 可能更平均化，crossover 比例可能低于预期\n\n## MVP 后续路径规划\n**PASS 路径**: 进入 research 阶段 → 写 paper_framing.md + experiment_plan.yaml → dream_simulate → 扩模型 (加 Llama/Mistral) + 扩 benchmark + ablation\n**FAIL 路径**: 分析失败原因 → (a) crossover 不存在: 假设被否定，pivot 或归档; (b) crossover 存在但 MCKP 增益 <0.5 PPL: 检查 solver/distortion metric 是否有改进空间，考虑换 distortion metric 或调 action set 粒度\n\n## 关键决策日志\n\n| ID | 日期 | 摘要 | 关联实验 |\n|----|------|------|----------|\n| D003 | 2026-05-13 | MVP 模型从 Llama 3.1-8B 切换到 Qwen2.5-7B，DuoAttention v | — |\n| D002 | 2026-05-13 | MVP gate v2: 加 quant-only MCKP baseline + 消除死区 + 最 | — |\n| D001 | 2026-05-13 | MVP 验证方案: Llama 3.1-8B 上验证 eviction-quantization c | — |\n\n\n",

From the head output, the first entry is about Senjō no Valkyria 3 and looks like ~600 chars. But many entries toward the end are shorter paragraphs. The Qwen tokenizer typically has ~3-4 chars per token for English text, so 512 tokens would need roughly 1500-2000 characters minimum. Many of these entries are clearly shorter than that.


Let me pull up the recent logs to see what's been happening.
